## 1. antd 호환성 fix
1-1. **올바른 원인 진단인가?** antd 6.x의 theme namespace가 "use client" + transitively createContext를 module top-level에서 호출 → server component에서 evaluate 시 에러. tailwind-bridge.ts에서 hardcoded fallback으로 우회.
1-2. **hardcoded SSR fallback이 antd v6.4.3 default seed token + defaultAlgorithm 결과와 정확히 일치하는가?**
   - light: colorPrimary=#1677FF, colorBgLayout=#F5F5F5, colorBgContainer=#FFFFFF, colorText=rgba(0,0,0,0.88), colorTextSecondary=rgba(0,0,0,0.65), colorBorder=#D9D9D9, borderRadius=6, boxShadowSecondary=...
   - dark: colorPrimary=#1668DC, colorBgLayout=#000000, colorBgContainer=#141414, colorText=rgba(255,255,255,0.85), colorTextSecondary=rgba(255,255,255,0.65), colorBorder=#424242
   antd source code(node_modules/antd/es/theme/...)에서 실제 default token 값 확인 후 비교.
1-3. **algorithms.ts "use client" marker 적절성** — algorithm 함수 객체가 server component(layout.tsx)에서 import는 가능하나 evaluation 시 placeholder. AntdRegistry/ConfigProvider(client)에서 unwrap. 정상 작동?
1-4. **layout.tsx 변경 정합성** — `getResolvedBridgeVarsByAppearance(appearance)` 호출이 light/dark cssVars 정확히 반환 + html style에 inject. 기존 동작과 visual regression?
1-5. **getResolvedBridgeVars(themeConfig) deprecated 처리** — 호출자가 있는지 grep으로 확인. tailwind-bridge.ts에서 backward-compatible export 유지하지만 themeConfig 무시. 위험?
1-6. **첫 페인트(SSR) FOUC 위험** — hardcoded fallback은 default brand color만. 사용자 dynamic brand override 시 차이. 현재 코드 brand override 있는지?

## 2. pg_cron 마이그레이션
2-1. **idempotency** — 동일 jobname 재실행 시 unschedule 후 재등록. multi-apply 안전?
2-2. **pg_cron 미설치 환경** — local Docker에 pg_cron 없을 수 있음. raise notice + return. 마이그레이션 자체 fail X?
2-3. **권한** — Supabase Cloud에서 cron.schedule 호출은 postgres role 전용. 마이그레이션은 어떤 role로 실행되는가? cron job command (private.cleanup_unconfirmed_users) 실행 role은? SECURITY DEFINER 함수가 정상 동작?
2-4. **schedule expression** — `0 4 * * *`가 매일 04:00 UTC. Supabase pg_cron이 UTC 기본?
2-5. **dollar-quoted string** — `$sql$ ... $sql$` 사용. command 안에 single quote가 없으므로 OK 같지만 nested quoting 안전성?
2-6. **함수 인자 default** — `private.cleanup_unconfirmed_users()` 호출 = retention_days=30, dry_run=false, max_batch=1000. 의도대로?
2-7. **다른 위험** — extension 활성화 누락, cron.job 테이블 직접 query 권한, jobname 중복 등.

1:# AI Development Workflow
2:
3:Entry point for every AI agent working in this repository. **Read this file first, then follow the links to the sub-doc that governs your current task.** Detailed rules live in the four sub-docs listed at the bottom â€” they are not duplicated here.
4:
5:## Mandatory Startup
6:
7:1. Invoke `using-superpowers` (Claude Code) or read its canonical SKILL file (other hosts). If host mirrors are stale, run `node scripts/sync-agent-skills.mjs` and retry.
8:2. Read [`docs/agent-index.md`](agent-index.md), classify your goal, and select the smallest matching docs.
9:3. Record `Docs consulted`, `Extracted requirements`, `Doc conflicts`, `Untouched relevant docs`, and `Context ledger` in your plan or ledger.
10:4. Run `node scripts/ai-workflow-check.mjs --repo .` before final reporting when Node is available. PRs run the same checker via `.github/workflows/ai-workflow-check.yml`.
11:
12:## Workflow Diagram
13:
14:```mermaid
15:flowchart TD
16:  A["Task starts"] --> B["Use Superpowers"]
17:  B --> C["Read agent-index"]
18:  C --> D["Select docs"]
19:  D --> E["Record evidence"]
20:  E --> F{"Covered by docs?"}
21:  F -->|"No"| X["Proposal or approved brief"]
22:  F -->|"Conflict"| Y["Stop with references"]
23:  F -->|"Yes"| G{"Lane"}
24:  G -->|"Tiny docs/config"| L["Lightweight path"]
25:  G -->|"Implementation"| P["Plan + TDD"]
26:  G -->|"UI/flow"| U["Plan + design QA"]
27:  P --> R["Review gates"]
28:  U --> R
29:  L --> V["Narrow verification"]
30:  R --> V
31:  V --> W["Workflow checker"]
32:  W --> Z["Final report"]
33:```

57:## Core Invariants
58:
59:These are mandatory for any non-lightweight change. **The linked sub-doc is the authoritative source** â€” this list exists so an agent reading only the entry file does not miss them.
60:
61:- **TDD** (RED â†’ confirm fail â†’ GREEN â†’ confirm pass â†’ refactor while green). Allowed exceptions are docs-only, config-only, generated artifacts, or no runnable test surface. Full loop and exceptions: [`review-gates.md#tdd`](ai-workflow/review-gates.md).
62:- **Cross-model review is mandatory** for every code change and every non-trivial plan or doc change. When only one model is available, record `Cross-model review: degraded â€” <reason>` in the ledger. [`review-gates.md#cross-model-review`](ai-workflow/review-gates.md#cross-model-review).
63:- **Plan-Review PASS Gate** â€” if a pre-implementation review (`plan-eng-review`, `codex consult`, etc) returns FAIL, revise the plan AND re-run the same review until PASS or until each remaining concern is recorded as "accepted with reason" in the ledger. [`review-gates.md#plan-review-pass-gate`](ai-workflow/review-gates.md).
64:- **Architecture Pass** at phase completion: route handlers have no leaked business logic, folder/name boundaries match `docs/domain-glossary.md`, no single concept is implemented in two places. [`review-gates.md#architecture-pass`](ai-workflow/review-gates.md).
65:- **UX/UI Consistency Pass** when changed files match UI patterns (`src/app/**`, `src/components/**`, `src/features/**`, `src/lib/ui/**`, `src/styles/**`, `*.css`, `theme*`, etc.). 4-line evidence(Tokens Â· Components Â· A11y Â· Responsive) in ledger, machine-checked. Test-only changes auto-exempt. [`review-gates.md#uxui-consistency-pass`](ai-workflow/review-gates.md).
66:- **CSS Variable Scoping Gate** when changed files include `src/theme/**`, `src/styles/**`, `app/layout.tsx`, or any file containing `--app-*` declarations: verify the five constraints in [`docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md#css-variable-scoping`](ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md) and run `scripts/ai-workflow-check.mjs`. The gate is machine-enforced for the following patterns: `--app-*: var(--ant-*)` chains, bare `@theme {` without `inline`, and `getAppTheme` at module scope. [`docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md#css-variable-architecture-contract`](ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md).
67:- **Light Spec + Out of Scope + Smallest Buildable Unit + Subagent-eligible column** are mandatory plan/light-spec sections, machine-checked by `scripts/ai-workflow-check.mjs`. [`planning-contracts.md`](ai-workflow/planning-contracts.md).
68:- **Context ledger** is required for any non-trivial work (multi-file, implementation, UI/route/auth/database/API/dependency/test-strategy/AI-boundary change, multi-agent work, work likely to resume across sessions, **or any change to workflow-governing files â€” `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/agent-index.md`, `docs/ai-development-workflow.md`, files under `docs/ai-workflow/`, `scripts/`, `.github/`**). [`context-and-packets.md`](ai-workflow/context-and-packets.md).
69:- **Fallback Protocol** â€” fallback never weakens a quality gate. Classify failures (fail-closed, degraded-mode, recover, retry-once, reassign) and record evidence in the ledger. [`fallback-and-recovery.md`](ai-workflow/fallback-and-recovery.md).
70:
71:## Required Evidence (Before Final Report)
72:
73:- `Docs consulted` â€” exact files read
74:- `Extracted requirements` â€” concrete requirements pulled from those files
75:- `Doc conflicts` â€” `none` or exact file references
76:- `Untouched relevant docs` â€” and why they were not read
77:- `Context ledger` â€” path or allowed lightweight exception
78:- Verification commands run and results
79:- Git publication decision per [`git-publication-decision.md`](ai-workflow/git-publication-decision.md)
80:
81:Final response must follow [`report-template.md`](ai-workflow/report-template.md).
82:
83:## Lightweight Path
84:
85:For a tiny docs/config/non-behavioral change with no multi-agent work, no UI/flow change, no doc conflict, and no resume risk:
86:
87:1. `using-superpowers`
88:2. Skill applicability check
89:3. Edit
90:4. Narrowest relevant verification (lint/typecheck/inspection)
91:5. Report checks and risks
92:
93:A context ledger may be skipped only if every condition above is satisfied. State the exception in the final report. This path is **not allowed for production behavior changes**.
94:

1:# AI Agent Index
2:
3:This is the mandatory routing index for AI agents. Read this file after `AGENTS.md` and before planning or editing.
4:
5:Purpose: make document access predictable as the project grows. Do not read every document by default. Select the smallest required set by task goal, then record the exact files in `Docs consulted`.
6:
7:## Startup Algorithm
8:
9:```mermaid
10:flowchart TD
11:    A["Read AGENTS.md"] --> B["Read docs/agent-index.md"]
12:    B --> C["Classify user goal"]
13:    C --> D["Read required docs for that goal"]
14:    D --> E["Record Docs consulted"]
15:    E --> F{"Covered by active docs?"}
16:    F -->|"Yes"| G["Plan or implement through workflow"]
17:    F -->|"No / conflict"| H["Stop for docs update proposal or user-approved brief"]
18:```
19:
20:## Index Groups
21:
22:| Index | Group | Entry point | Use for |
23:| --- | --- | --- | --- |
24:| `ROOT-00` | Human project map | [../README.md](../README.md) | Human-friendly project overview |

26:| `AGENT-00` | AI routing | [agent-index.md](agent-index.md) | Required AI document selection |
27:| `FLOW-00` | AI workflow entry | [ai-development-workflow.md](ai-development-workflow.md) | Entry point â€” 88-line index that links sub-docs |
28:| `WF-00` | Workflow sub-docs | [ai-workflow/planning-contracts.md](ai-workflow/planning-contracts.md), [ai-workflow/context-and-packets.md](ai-workflow/context-and-packets.md), [ai-workflow/review-gates.md](ai-workflow/review-gates.md), [ai-workflow/fallback-and-recovery.md](ai-workflow/fallback-and-recovery.md) | Planning Â· ledger/packets Â· review gates Â· fallback matrix |

30:| `HARNESS-00` | Harness and skills | [ai-workflow/harness-and-skills.md](ai-workflow/harness-and-skills.md) | TALKPIK, practical skills, GStack, Superpowers, host-specific skill names |
31:| `PRODUCT-00` | Product requirements | [prd.md](prd.md) | Product scope, users, value, business rules |

40:## Goal-To-Doc Routing
41:
42:| User goal | Required docs | Conditional docs | Notes |
43:| --- | --- | --- | --- |
44:| Product scope, user value, roles, business direction | `PRODUCT-00` | `SPEC-00`, `JOURNEY-00` | Do not invent new product direction. |
45:| Functional behavior, validation, data handling, acceptance criteria | `SPEC-00` | `PRODUCT-00`, `JOURNEY-00` | Use for implementation and tests. |
46:| Framework, package, dependency, backend, auth, deployment, env vars, deferred billing | `SPEC-00` | Matching `DEV-10` detail file | Start at `spec.md`; do not read all development details by default. |
47:| Navigation, routes, page hierarchy | `IA-00` | `JOURNEY-00`, `IA-10` | Use Target React Route Map, not legacy route notes. |
48:| User journey, screen order, transitions, entry/exit states | `JOURNEY-00` | `IA-10`, `SPEC-00` | `docs/user-flow.md` is legacy context only. |
49:| Visual UI, layout, components, tokens, motion | `UI-00` | `IA-10`, `JOURNEY-00` | Run design review before user-facing implementation. |
50:| Specific page or screen | `IA-10` matching page | `UI-00`, `JOURNEY-00`, `SPEC-00` | Read the matching `description.md` and inspect `wireframe.png`. |
51:| Admin í™”ë©´ / ê¶Œí•œ / RBAC / audit log | `SPEC-00`, í•´ë‹¹ admin `IA-10`(`docs/IA/{21-H-01,30-X-08,32-X-10}-*`), `DEV-10`(backend-auth) | `JOURNEY-00`, `UI-00`, `docs/ai-workflow/light-specs/phase-6-admin-library-hardening.md` | Adminì€ ì¼ë°˜ ì‚¬ìš©ìžì™€ ê¶Œí•œ ëª¨ë¸Â·UI ë¶„ê¸°Â·QA ì‹œë‚˜ë¦¬ì˜¤ê°€ ë‹¤ë¦„. 52:| AI workflow, context, reports, fallback, multi-agent work | `FLOW-00`, `WF-00` | `HARNESS-00` | Required for workflow or harness changes. |
53:| Historical page composition | `LEGACY-00` | Active docs above | Reference only. Active docs win. |
54:
55:## Development Detail Routing
56:
57:Use this table only after reading [spec.md](spec.md).
58:
59:| Development goal | Read |
60:| --- | --- |
61:| Frontend framework, package choice, runtime, UI library, forms, validation, charts, tests | [development/stack.md](development/stack.md) |
62:| Supabase, Auth, Postgres, RLS, Storage, server-only keys | [development/backend-auth.md](development/backend-auth.md) |
63:| Vercel, environments, deployment gates, environment variables, rollback, CI, preview links | [development/deployment.md](development/deployment.md) |
64:| Billing, subscription, paywall, payment provider, deferred scope | [development/deferred-scope.md](development/deferred-scope.md) |
65:
66:## Active Vs Legacy Rule
67:
68:Active docs govern implementation, QA, and review:
69:
70:- [prd.md](prd.md)
71:- [spec.md](spec.md)
72:- [ant-design/README.md](ant-design/README.md)
73:- [sitemap.md](sitemap.md) Target React Route Map
74:- [ia.md](ia.md)
75:- [IA/README.md](IA/README.md) and matching `docs/IA/<page>/description.md`
76:- [flow/user-flow.md](flow/user-flow.md)
77:
78:Legacy docs are reference only:
79:
80:- [user-flow.md](user-flow.md)
81:- [ia-pages/README.md](ia-pages/README.md)
82:- Legacy HTML Route Map sections inside [sitemap.md](sitemap.md)
83:
84:If active and legacy docs conflict, active docs win. If active docs conflict with the user request, stop and report the conflict.
85:
86:## Ledger Requirement Index
87:
88:Create or update a run ledger under `docs/ai-workflow/runs/YYYY/MM/DD/` when any of these apply:
89:
90:| Trigger | Required |
91:| --- | --- |
92:| Non-trivial work | Yes |
93:| Implementation work | Yes |
94:| UI, route, flow, or integration changes | Yes |
95:| Net-new scope or doc conflict | Yes |
96:| Multi-agent work | Yes |
97:| Work likely to resume later | Yes |
98:| Tiny docs/config-only edit with no behavior change, no conflict, no multi-agent work, no resume risk | May skip with reason |
99:
100:Use [ai-workflow/context-ledger-template.md](ai-workflow/context-ledger-template.md).
101:
102:For consistency, classify the work as non-trivial when it changes workflow-governing files, automation, scripts, CI, routes, UI, auth, database, API, dependencies, deployment behavior, test strategy, or AI-service boundaries. Multi-file changes are non-trivial by default unless they are purely mechanical docs/config updates and the final report states the lightweight exception.
103:
104:When a ledger is required, update it before final verification and include it in the dirty scope, commit trailers, PR body, or final report as applicable.
105:
106:## Output Requirement
107:
108:Every plan, handoff, or final report must include:
109:
110:- `Docs consulted`: exact files read.
111:- `Extracted requirements`: concrete requirements taken from those docs.
112:- `Doc conflicts`: `none` or exact conflict references.
113:- `Untouched relevant docs`: docs that seemed related but were not read, with reason.
114:- `Context ledger`: path or allowed lightweight exception.

tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:50:  **Author's proposed resolution: drop the `admin_audit_logs` insert entirely.** Rationale: `pg_cron` already records every run in `cron.job_run_details` with `start_time`, `end_time`, `status`, `return_message`. Adding a second audit row for system-initiated cleanup duplicates state and forces us to invent a synthetic actor or change schema. Operational visibility is preserved via `cron.job_run_details`. The function will instead `RAISE LOG` the deleted count, which Supabase ships to logs.
tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:54:- **S8a (pg_cron extension syntax).** ACCEPTED. Will use Supabase's documented pattern: enable via Dashboard, then `create extension if not exists pg_cron;` (no explicit schema clause).

tasks\codex-prompt-email-confirmation-review-round2.md:38:  **Author's proposed resolution: drop the `admin_audit_logs` insert entirely.** Rationale: `pg_cron` already records every run in `cron.job_run_details` with `start_time`, `end_time`, `status`, `return_message`. Adding a second audit row for system-initiated cleanup duplicates state and forces us to invent a synthetic actor or change schema. Operational visibility is preserved via `cron.job_run_details`. The function will instead `RAISE LOG` the deleted count, which Supabase ships to logs.

tasks\codex-runs\stderr-2-playwright.txt:649:tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:50:  **Author's proposed resolution: drop the `admin_audit_logs` insert entirely.** Rationale: `pg_cron` already records every run in `cron.job_run_details` with `start_time`, `end_time`, `status`, `return_message`. Adding a second audit row for system-initiated cleanup duplicates state and forces us to invent a synthetic actor or change schema. Operational visibility is preserved via `cron.job_run_details`. The function will instead `RAISE LOG` the deleted count, which Supabase ships to logs.

docs/development/backend-auth.md:1:# Backend And Authentication
docs/development/backend-auth.md:2:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:3:> Last updated: 2026-05-19
docs/development/backend-auth.md:4:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:5:This file fixes the backend, database, and authentication choices for TALKPIK AI.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:6:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:7:## Backend Stack
docs/development/backend-auth.md:8:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:9:| Area | Fixed Choice | Version Policy | Reason |
docs/development/backend-auth.md:10:| --- | --- | --- | --- |
docs/development/backend-auth.md:11:| Backend platform | `Supabase` | managed current stable | Auth, Postgres, RLS, Storage, generated APIs. |
docs/development/backend-auth.md:12:| DB client | `@supabase/supabase-js` | `2.x` | Official client for browser/server Supabase access. |
docs/development/backend-auth.md:13:| SSR auth helper | `@supabase/ssr` | latest stable `0.x` until 1.x exists | Cookie-based auth for Next.js server/client boundaries. |
docs/development/backend-auth.md:14:| Database | Supabase Postgres | managed stable | Relational learning, attempts, feedback, profile, and admin data. |
docs/development/backend-auth.md:15:| Authorization | Supabase RLS | mandatory | User-owned learning data must be protected at the database layer. |
docs/development/backend-auth.md:16:| Storage | Supabase Storage | managed stable | Avatars, generated PDFs, exported feedback, and future media. |
docs/development/backend-auth.md:17:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:18:## Backend Rules
docs/development/backend-auth.md:19:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:20:- Default data access goes through Supabase and RLS.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:21:- New tables in exposed schemas must enable RLS before user access.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:22:- Do not use `service_role` keys in client code.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:23:- Keep `service_role` usage server-only and narrowly scoped.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:24:- Store authorization-critical role/plan data in trusted server/database fields, not user-editable metadata.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:25:- Start without Prisma/Drizzle. Use SQL migrations and generated Supabase types first.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:26:- Add an ORM only after there is a concrete problem SQL + Supabase types cannot solve.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:27:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:28:## Authentication
docs/development/backend-auth.md:29:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:30:Default provider: `Supabase Auth`.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:31:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:32:Reason:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:33:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:34:- The product is data-heavy and user-owned.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:35:- Supabase Auth integrates directly with Supabase RLS.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:36:- The app needs profile, goals, learning progress, writing drafts, feedback, and admin access.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:37:- A single identity plane is simpler for MVP and safer for RLS.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:38:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:39:```mermaid
docs/development/backend-auth.md:40:flowchart LR
docs/development/backend-auth.md:41:    A["Supabase Auth user"] --> P["profiles row"]
docs/development/backend-auth.md:42:    P --> G["learning_goals"]
docs/development/backend-auth.md:43:    P --> R["role / admin flags"]
docs/development/backend-auth.md:44:    R --> RLS["RLS policies"]
docs/development/backend-auth.md:45:    RLS --> DATA["user-owned learning data"]
docs/development/backend-auth.md:46:```
docs/development/backend-auth.md:47:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:48:## Clerk Decision
docs/development/backend-auth.md:49:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:50:Clerk is not the default. Reconsider Clerk only if one of these becomes a
docs/development/backend-auth.md:51:near-term requirement:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:52:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:53:- enterprise SSO,
docs/development/backend-auth.md:54:- organization/team membership as a core feature,
docs/development/backend-auth.md:55:- hosted auth UI is more important than database-native authorization,
docs/development/backend-auth.md:56:- B2B account administration becomes central to the product.
docs/development/backend-auth.md:57:
docs/development/backend-auth.md:58:If Clerk is reconsidered, create a stack-change note before implementation.
docs/spec.md:1:# TALKPIK AI Implementation Spec
docs/spec.md:2:
docs/spec.md:3:> Status: single required entry point for implementation decisions
docs/spec.md:4:>
docs/spec.md:5:> Last updated: 2026-05-19
docs/spec.md:6:
docs/spec.md:7:This is the canonical implementation spec for TALKPIK AI. Read this file first
docs/spec.md:8:for any development, dependency, frontend, backend, auth, AI, deployment,
docs/spec.md:9:environment, route, or test task. Then read only the matching detailed documents
docs/spec.md:10:listed below.
docs/spec.md:11:
docs/spec.md:12:This repository is currently pre-implementation. There is no stable `src/` or
docs/spec.md:13:`package.json` yet. Treat this document and the linked active docs as the target
docs/spec.md:14:implementation contract until production source exists. After production source
docs/spec.md:15:exists, reconcile accepted docs with current source before changing behavior.
docs/spec.md:16:
docs/spec.md:17:## Fixed Baseline
docs/spec.md:18:
docs/spec.md:19:| Area | Fixed decision |
docs/spec.md:20:| --- | --- |
docs/spec.md:21:| Application architecture | Frontend + serverless application |
docs/spec.md:22:| Application framework | `Next.js App Router` |
docs/spec.md:23:| UI runtime | `React` |
docs/spec.md:24:| Language | `TypeScript` |
docs/spec.md:25:| UI system | `Ant Design` with `ConfigProvider` and theme tokens |
docs/spec.md:26:| Styling utilities | `Tailwind CSS` as a constrained utility layer |
docs/spec.md:27:| Backend | `Supabase` as the serverless backend platform |
docs/spec.md:28:| Database | Supabase-hosted `Postgres` |
docs/spec.md:29:| Auth | `Supabase Auth` |
docs/spec.md:30:| Storage | `Supabase Storage` |
docs/spec.md:31:| Deployment | `Vercel` as the serverless deployment target |
docs/spec.md:32:| Package manager | `pnpm` |
docs/spec.md:33:| Billing | Deferred; not part of the current fixed stack |
docs/spec.md:34:
docs/spec.md:35:## Required Reading Map
docs/spec.md:36:
docs/spec.md:37:Read this file first, then select the smallest matching set.
docs/spec.md:38:
docs/spec.md:39:| Work mentions | Also read |
docs/spec.md:40:| --- | --- |
docs/spec.md:41:| framework, package, dependency, library, UI stack, state, forms, validation, chart, test | `docs/development/stack.md` |
docs/spec.md:42:| Supabase, database, auth, login, RLS, storage, profile, admin role, server key | `docs/development/backend-auth.md` |
docs/spec.md:43:| table, column, schema, migration, DDL, RLS policy SQL, index | `docs/development/database-schema.md` |
docs/spec.md:44:| Vercel, deploy, deployment, preview, production, environment variable, rollback, CI | `docs/development/deployment.md` |
docs/spec.md:45:| billing, subscription, paywall, payment, Stripe, plan pricing | `docs/development/deferred-scope.md` |
docs/spec.md:46:| page, route, navigation, user flow | `docs/sitemap.md`, `docs/ia.md`, `docs/flow/user-flow.md`, and matching `docs/IA/<page>/description.md` when page-specific |
docs/spec.md:47:| visual UI, Ant Design component, theme, layout, motion | `docs/ant-design/README.md` and the matching Ant Design detail docs it routes to |
docs/spec.md:48:
docs/spec.md:49:Do not read every detailed document by default. Use this map to keep the context
docs/spec.md:50:small and the implementation grounded.
docs/spec.md:51:
docs/spec.md:52:## Source Structure
docs/spec.md:53:
docs/spec.md:54:Use Next.js App Router conventions once source exists.
docs/spec.md:55:
docs/spec.md:56:```text
docs/spec.md:57:src/
docs/spec.md:58:  app/
docs/spec.md:59:    layout.tsx
docs/spec.md:60:    page.tsx
docs/spec.md:61:    (workspace)/
docs/spec.md:62:    api/
docs/spec.md:63:  components/
docs/spec.md:64:    app/
docs/spec.md:65:    shared/
docs/spec.md:66:  lib/
docs/spec.md:67:    supabase/
docs/spec.md:68:    validation/
docs/spec.md:69:  stores/
docs/spec.md:70:  styles/
docs/spec.md:71:  theme/
docs/spec.md:72:  types/
docs/spec.md:73:```
docs/spec.md:74:
docs/spec.md:75:Folder responsibilities:
docs/spec.md:76:
docs/spec.md:77:- `src/app/`: route tree, layouts, route handlers, loading/error boundaries, and
docs/spec.md:78:  server actions where appropriate.
docs/spec.md:79:- `src/components/app/`: app shell pieces such as sidebar, header, and settings.
docs/spec.md:80:- `src/components/shared/`: reusable UI blocks shared by multiple pages.
docs/spec.md:81:- `src/lib/`: Supabase clients, validation schemas, and server-only utilities.
docs/spec.md:82:- `src/stores/`: focused Zustand stores for recoverable client interaction state.
docs/spec.md:83:- `src/styles/`: Tailwind entrypoint and minimal global CSS used only where
docs/spec.md:84:  layout glue is necessary.
docs/spec.md:85:- `src/theme/`: Ant Design theme setup, token composition, theme presets, and
docs/spec.md:86:  the Tailwind token bridge.
docs/spec.md:87:- `src/types/`: shared TypeScript types.
docs/spec.md:88:
docs/spec.md:89:Do not use `src/App.tsx` as the route authority. The current route authority is
docs/spec.md:90:`docs/sitemap.md` until source exists; after implementation starts, the Next.js
docs/spec.md:91:`src/app/` route tree becomes the implementation reference.
docs/spec.md:92:
docs/spec.md:93:## Frontend Implementation Rules
docs/spec.md:94:
docs/spec.md:95:- Use Ant Design components before building custom UI.
docs/spec.md:96:- Use Ant Design theme tokens before hardcoded colors, shadows, radii, or spacing.
docs/spec.md:97:- Use Tailwind CSS for constrained utility styling, responsive layout glue, and
docs/spec.md:98:  small one-off adjustments when Ant Design components or tokens are not enough.
docs/spec.md:99:- Do not use Tailwind as the design system, component library, or source of
docs/spec.md:100:  brand tokens. Ant Design tokens remain the styling authority.
docs/spec.md:101:- Keep theme decisions centralized under `src/theme/`.
docs/spec.md:102:- Keep Tailwind and Ant Design visually synchronized through shared theme CSS
docs/spec.md:103:  variables generated from the active Ant Design theme. Do not copy separate
docs/spec.md:104:  Tailwind color, radius, shadow, font, or spacing values by hand.
docs/spec.md:105:- Use `ConfigProvider` at the app root.
docs/spec.md:106:- Use Ant Design `App` provider for message, notification, and modal context.
docs/spec.md:107:- Prefer Ant Design layout and feedback primitives such as `Layout`, `Row`,
docs/spec.md:108:  `Col`, `Flex`, `Space`, `Card`, `Form`, `Table`, `Tabs`, `Drawer`, `Modal`,
docs/spec.md:109:  `Descriptions`, `Statistic`, `Alert`, `Result`, and `Progress`.
docs/spec.md:110:- Do not introduce deprecated Ant Design components in new UI work.
docs/spec.md:111:- Include loading, empty, error, success, and disabled states.
docs/spec.md:112:- Verify desktop and mobile layout before calling UI work complete.
docs/spec.md:113:- Keep route-level pages thin; domain logic belongs in focused helpers, stores,
docs/spec.md:114:  server actions, route handlers, or API modules.
docs/spec.md:115:- Do not introduce shadcn/ui, Chakra, MUI, Redux, or MobX without a written
docs/spec.md:116:  stack-change decision and user approval or an updated spec.
docs/spec.md:117:
docs/spec.md:118:Detailed UI policy lives in `docs/ant-design/`.
docs/spec.md:119:
docs/spec.md:120:## State Management Model
docs/spec.md:121:
docs/spec.md:122:Use the smallest state owner that fits the data lifecycle:
docs/spec.md:123:
docs/spec.md:124:- Keep route and data fetching decisions in Next.js Server Components, route
docs/spec.md:125:  handlers, or server actions where practical.
docs/spec.md:126:- Use React local state for component-private UI state.
docs/spec.md:127:- Use URL search params for shareable filters, tabs, pagination, or route-level
docs/spec.md:128:  view state.
docs/spec.md:129:- Use React Hook Form for form-local state and validation flow.
docs/spec.md:130:- Use TanStack Query only for client-side server state that cannot stay purely
docs/spec.md:131:  server-rendered, including cache, mutation, retry, and invalidation state.
docs/spec.md:132:- Use Zustand only for client interaction state that benefits from local
docs/spec.md:133:  recovery, cross-component coordination, or temporary UI continuity.
docs/spec.md:134:- Do not duplicate server-derived data in Zustand unless the UI needs a
docs/spec.md:135:  temporary editable draft or optimistic interaction state.
docs/spec.md:136:
docs/spec.md:137:Target stores:
docs/spec.md:138:
docs/spec.md:139:- `useUserStore`: learner profile display state, plan, language, and goal basics.
docs/spec.md:140:- `useLearningStore`: dashboard learning metrics and current progress UI state.
docs/spec.md:141:- `usePracticeStore`: reading/listening practice solving state.
docs/spec.md:142:- `useWritingStore`: writing setup, draft, autosave, and submission flow.
docs/spec.md:143:- `useFeedbackStore`: writing feedback list/detail UI state.
docs/spec.md:144:- `useThemeStore`: light/dark theme preference.
docs/spec.md:145:
docs/spec.md:146:Draft-like user input must be recoverable. Long-form writing surfaces need
docs/spec.md:147:autosave or clear draft-preservation cues.
docs/spec.md:148:
docs/spec.md:149:## Backend And Auth Rules
docs/spec.md:150:
docs/spec.md:151:- Use Supabase as the serverless backend platform.
docs/spec.md:152:- Use Supabase-hosted Postgres for relational data.
docs/spec.md:153:- Use Supabase Auth for authentication.
docs/spec.md:154:- Use Supabase Storage for user files or generated exports when storage is
docs/spec.md:155:  required.
docs/spec.md:156:- Enforce data access with RLS policies.
docs/spec.md:157:- Keep server-only keys on the server. Never expose Supabase `service_role` or
docs/spec.md:158:  other secrets in browser-visible variables.
docs/spec.md:159:- Use browser-visible Supabase variables only for publishable configuration.
docs/spec.md:160:- Read `docs/development/backend-auth.md` before implementing auth, RLS,
docs/spec.md:161:  storage, profile, or admin-role behavior.
docs/spec.md:162:
docs/spec.md:163:## Serverless Architecture Rules
docs/spec.md:164:
docs/spec.md:165:- Build the project as a frontend + serverless application.
docs/spec.md:166:- Treat Supabase and Vercel as the fixed serverless stack for backend capability,
docs/spec.md:167:  runtime boundaries, hosting, deployment, and environment management.
docs/spec.md:168:- Prefer managed Supabase capabilities and Vercel-supported Next.js serverless
docs/spec.md:169:  boundaries over self-managed backend servers.
docs/spec.md:170:- Do not introduce a separate long-running backend service without an approved
docs/spec.md:171:  architecture decision and updated spec.
docs/spec.md:172:
docs/spec.md:173:## Deployment And Environment Rules
docs/spec.md:174:
docs/spec.md:175:- Deploy on Vercel as the serverless deployment target.
docs/spec.md:176:- Use `pnpm` and commit `pnpm-lock.yaml` once packages exist.
docs/spec.md:177:- Configure Vercel project environments before sharing Preview links.
docs/spec.md:178:- Keep secrets out of committed files.
docs/spec.md:179:- Keep environment-variable names and visibility aligned with
docs/spec.md:180:  `docs/development/deployment.md`.
docs/spec.md:181:- Read `docs/development/deployment.md` before deployment, preview, production,
docs/spec.md:182:  rollback, CI, or environment-variable work.
docs/spec.md:183:
docs/spec.md:184:## Testing And Quality
docs/spec.md:185:
docs/spec.md:186:Once `package.json` exists, the implementation must provide scripts for:
docs/spec.md:187:
docs/spec.md:188:- `dev`
docs/spec.md:189:- `build`
docs/spec.md:190:- `start`
docs/spec.md:191:- `lint`
docs/spec.md:192:- `typecheck`
docs/spec.md:193:- `test`
docs/spec.md:194:- `test:e2e`
docs/spec.md:195:- `format`
docs/spec.md:196:
docs/spec.md:197:Follow the exact script expectations in `docs/development/stack.md` unless that
docs/spec.md:198:file is updated by an approved stack-change decision.
docs/spec.md:199:
docs/spec.md:200:Before calling implementation work complete:
docs/spec.md:201:
docs/spec.md:202:- run the relevant focused tests,
docs/spec.md:203:- run lint/typecheck/build when available and proportionate,
docs/spec.md:204:- check desktop and mobile layout for UI work,
docs/spec.md:205:- apply the Ant Design review checklist for UI work,
docs/spec.md:206:- run browser or visual QA for user-facing flows,
docs/spec.md:207:- and report what works, what does not work yet, and what risk remains.
docs/spec.md:208:
docs/spec.md:209:## Non-Negotiable Rules
docs/spec.md:210:
docs/spec.md:211:- Do not skip this file for implementation work.
docs/spec.md:212:- Do not add framework-level dependencies without a stack-change note and user
docs/spec.md:213:  approval or an updated spec.
docs/spec.md:214:- Do not add billing SDKs or payment flows unless billing scope is explicitly
docs/spec.md:215:  reopened.
docs/spec.md:216:- Do not expose secrets in browser-visible variables.
docs/spec.md:217:- Do not share Preview links until Vercel environment variables are configured.
docs/spec.md:218:- Do not treat legacy static `.html` route notes as current implementation
docs/spec.md:219:  targets.
docs/spec.md:220:- Do not silently invent product behavior. Use `docs/prd.md`, `docs/sitemap.md`,
docs/spec.md:221:  `docs/ia.md`, `docs/flow/user-flow.md`, and matching IA page docs for product,
docs/spec.md:222:  route, and flow requirements.
docs/spec.md:223:
docs/spec.md:224:## Implementation Start Checklist
docs/spec.md:225:
docs/spec.md:226:Before creating app code:
docs/spec.md:227:
docs/spec.md:228:- Re-read this file.
docs/spec.md:229:- Read only the matching detailed files from the Required Reading Map.
docs/spec.md:230:- Re-read `docs/ai-development-workflow.md`.
docs/spec.md:231:- Re-read `docs/ant-design/README.md` for UI work.
docs/spec.md:232:- Use `docs/sitemap.md` as the route map until `src/app/` exists.
docs/spec.md:233:- Create `package.json` with `pnpm`.
docs/spec.md:234:- Pin versions through `pnpm-lock.yaml`.
docs/spec.md:235:- Configure Supabase env variables with publishable keys only in browser-visible
docs/spec.md:236:  variables.
docs/spec.md:237:- Configure Vercel project environments before sharing Preview links.
docs/spec.md:238:- Keep secrets out of committed files.
docs/spec.md:239:- Create initial tests before non-trivial implementation unless a documented TDD
docs/spec.md:240:  exception applies.
docs/spec.md:241:

docs/development/stack.md:1:# Development Stack
docs/development/stack.md:2:
docs/development/stack.md:3:> Last updated: 2026-05-19
docs/development/stack.md:4:
docs/development/stack.md:5:This file fixes the framework, runtime, package, and frontend library choices
docs/development/stack.md:6:for TALKPIK AI.
docs/development/stack.md:7:
docs/development/stack.md:8:## Runtime And Tooling
docs/development/stack.md:9:
docs/development/stack.md:10:| Area | Fixed Choice | Version Policy | Reason |
docs/development/stack.md:11:| --- | --- | --- | --- |
docs/development/stack.md:12:| Runtime | `Node.js` | `24.x LTS` | Current active LTS line; suitable for modern Next.js/Vercel work. |
docs/development/stack.md:13:| Package manager | `pnpm` | latest stable major at bootstrap | Deterministic lockfile, efficient installs, good monorepo path if needed later. |
docs/development/stack.md:14:| Framework | `next` | `16.x` | App Router, Route Handlers, Server Actions, serverless deployment fit. |
docs/development/stack.md:15:| UI runtime | `react`, `react-dom` | `19.x` | Current React line used by modern Next.js. |
docs/development/stack.md:16:| Language | `typescript` | `6.x` unless Next.js compatibility requires latest supported `5.x` | Strong domain models and API contracts. |
docs/development/stack.md:17:| Linting | `eslint` + `eslint-config-next` | latest stable major supported by Next.js | Required before completion. |
docs/development/stack.md:18:| Formatting | `prettier` | `3.x` | Consistent formatting without style debates. |
docs/development/stack.md:19:
docs/development/stack.md:20:Implementation rule:
docs/development/stack.md:21:
docs/development/stack.md:22:- The first `package.json` must include a `packageManager` field.
docs/development/stack.md:23:- The first lockfile must be `pnpm-lock.yaml`.
docs/development/stack.md:24:- Do not mix npm, yarn, and pnpm lockfiles.
docs/development/stack.md:25:
docs/development/stack.md:26:## Frontend Stack
docs/development/stack.md:27:
docs/development/stack.md:28:| Area | Fixed Choice | Version Policy | Reason |
docs/development/stack.md:29:| --- | --- | --- | --- |
docs/development/stack.md:30:| App framework | `Next.js App Router` | `16.x` | File-system routing, layouts, server components, route handlers. |
docs/development/stack.md:31:| Component system | `Ant Design` | `6.x` | Enterprise-grade app UI, forms, tables, layout, feedback, theme tokens. |
docs/development/stack.md:32:| Styling utilities | `tailwindcss` | `4.x` | Constrained utility layer for responsive layout glue and one-off styling. |
docs/development/stack.md:33:| CSS processing | `@tailwindcss/postcss` + `postcss` | same stable major as Tailwind / latest compatible `8.x` PostCSS | Tailwind v4 PostCSS integration for Next.js. |
docs/development/stack.md:34:| Icons | `lucide-react` | latest stable major | Simple consistent app icons where AntD icons are not enough. |
docs/development/stack.md:35:| Client state | `zustand` | `5.x` | Lightweight state for UI/task state. |
docs/development/stack.md:36:| Server state | `@tanstack/react-query` | `5.x` | Caching and mutation state for client-side data that cannot stay purely server-rendered. |
docs/development/stack.md:37:| Forms | `react-hook-form` | `7.x` | Complex forms with low render overhead. |
docs/development/stack.md:38:| Validation | `zod` | `4.x` | Shared schema validation for forms and API payloads. |
docs/development/stack.md:39:| Charts | `recharts` | `3.x` | Growth dashboard and learning progress visualization. Mock exam charts are future context unless matching IA/routes are reintroduced. |
docs/development/stack.md:40:| Dates | `dayjs` | `1.x` | Date formatting, exam date, subscription date, and learning history display. |
docs/development/stack.md:41:
docs/development/stack.md:42:Frontend rules:
docs/development/stack.md:43:
docs/development/stack.md:44:- Use Ant Design components before custom UI.
docs/development/stack.md:45:- Use Ant Design tokens before hardcoded colors, radius, spacing, or shadows.
docs/development/stack.md:46:- Use Tailwind CSS only as a constrained utility layer. Do not let utility
docs/development/stack.md:47:  classes replace Ant Design components, theme tokens, or centralized theme
docs/development/stack.md:48:  decisions.
docs/development/stack.md:49:- Tailwind theme values must come from the active Ant Design theme through a
docs/development/stack.md:50:  CSS-variable bridge. Do not maintain an independent Tailwind palette, radius
docs/development/stack.md:51:  scale, shadow scale, or font stack.
docs/development/stack.md:52:- Keep theme configuration under `src/theme/`.
docs/development/stack.md:53:- Use `ConfigProvider` at the app root.
docs/development/stack.md:54:- Use Ant Design `App` provider for message, notification, and modal context.
docs/development/stack.md:55:- Keep route-level pages thin; domain logic belongs in focused helpers, stores, server actions, or API modules.
docs/development/stack.md:56:- State ownership order: Server Components/server actions for server-owned data, React local state for component-private UI, URL search params for shareable view state, React Hook Form for form state, TanStack Query for client-side server state, and Zustand for recoverable cross-component client interaction state.
docs/development/stack.md:57:- Do not introduce shadcn/ui, Chakra, MUI, Redux, or MobX without a written stack-change decision.
docs/development/stack.md:58:
docs/development/stack.md:59:## Testing And Quality
docs/development/stack.md:60:
docs/development/stack.md:61:| Area | Fixed Choice | Version Policy | Reason |
docs/development/stack.md:62:| --- | --- | --- | --- |
docs/development/stack.md:63:| Unit tests | `vitest` | `4.x` | Fast TypeScript test runner. |
docs/development/stack.md:64:| UI component tests | `@testing-library/react` | latest stable major | User-oriented component assertions. |
docs/development/stack.md:65:| Browser/e2e tests | `playwright` | `1.x` | Required for route, auth, and visual workflow checks. |
docs/development/stack.md:66:| API validation tests | `vitest` + `zod` schemas | same as above | Validate contracts without requiring full browser flows. |
docs/development/stack.md:67:
docs/development/stack.md:68:Required scripts once `package.json` exists:
docs/development/stack.md:69:
docs/development/stack.md:70:```json
docs/development/stack.md:71:{
docs/development/stack.md:72:  "scripts": {
docs/development/stack.md:73:    "dev": "next dev",
docs/development/stack.md:74:    "build": "next build",
docs/development/stack.md:75:    "start": "next start",
docs/development/stack.md:76:    "lint": "eslint .",
docs/development/stack.md:77:    "typecheck": "tsc --noEmit",
docs/development/stack.md:78:    "test": "vitest run",
docs/development/stack.md:79:    "test:watch": "vitest",
docs/development/stack.md:80:    "test:e2e": "playwright test",
docs/development/stack.md:81:    "format": "prettier --check .",
docs/development/stack.md:82:    "format:write": "prettier --write ."
docs/development/stack.md:83:  }
docs/development/stack.md:84:}
docs/development/stack.md:85:```

docs/development/stack.md:119:## Rejected Defaults
docs/development/stack.md:120:
docs/development/stack.md:121:| Rejected | Reason |
docs/development/stack.md:122:| --- | --- |
docs/development/stack.md:123:| Firebase as primary backend | Product data is relational and benefits from Postgres/RLS. |
docs/development/stack.md:124:| Tailwind as the primary UI system | Local project spec remains Ant Design-first. |
docs/development/stack.md:125:| shadcn/ui | Historical references only; not the current implementation standard. |
docs/development/stack.md:126:| Redux Toolkit | Zustand is enough for focused client UI state. |
docs/development/stack.md:127:| Prisma/Drizzle at MVP start | Direct Supabase SQL/types are simpler and keep RLS visible. |
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:1:# AI Development Workflow
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:2:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:3:This file defines the required workflow for AI-assisted UI development in this
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:4:project.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:5:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:6:## Before Coding
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:7:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:8:1. Read the product docs in `docs/`.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:9:2. Read `docs/ant-design/README.md`.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:10:3. Read all files listed in the required reading order.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:11:4. Identify the page pattern:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:12:   - dashboard/workbench
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:13:   - form page
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:14:   - problem solving page
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:15:   - writing editor
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:16:   - list page
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:17:   - detail page
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:18:   - result page
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:19:   - exception page
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:20:   - exam workspace
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:21:5. Choose AntD components before writing custom UI.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:22:6. Complete the AntD component API mapping before implementation:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:23:   - extract visible data, states, actions, and layout roles from IA documents
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:24:     and user requirements
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:25:   - list the selected AntD components for each visible UI area
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:26:   - inspect each selected component's official API through AntD MCP, AntD CLI,
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:27:     AntD LLM-ready docs, or official component docs
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:28:   - map the extracted requirements to built-in props, slots, variants,
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:29:     semantic DOM hooks, and design tokens before custom implementation
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:30:   - record why any remaining custom markup, custom CSS, or custom interaction
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:31:     logic is necessary
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:32:   - do not proceed to UI implementation until this mapping is recorded
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:33:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:34:## During Coding
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:35:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:36:Use this order:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:37:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:38:1. Implement from the completed AntD component API mapping.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:39:2. Structure with AntD layout and components.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:40:3. Configure global theme tokens.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:41:4. Use component tokens for targeted customization.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:42:5. Add local CSS only for project layout glue.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:43:6. Add responsive behavior.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:44:7. Add loading, empty, success, error, and disabled states.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:45:8. Verify accessibility labels and keyboard behavior.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:46:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:47:## Overlay Workflow Rule
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:48:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:49:`src/`ê°€ ì•„ì§ ìƒì„±ë˜ì§€ ì•Šì€ pre-implementation ìƒíƒœì—ì„œëŠ” ì´ ê·œì¹™ì´ "ì²˜ìŒ ë§Œë“¤ ë•Œ
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:50:ì´ êµ¬ì¡°ë¡œ ë§Œë“¤ì–´ë¼"ë¥¼ ì˜ë¯¸í•©ë‹ˆë‹¤. `src/`ê°€ ìƒì„±ëœ ì´í›„ì—ëŠ” ê°•ì œ ê·œì¹™ìœ¼ë¡œ ì ìš©ë©ë‹ˆë‹¤.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:51:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:52:For user-facing surfaces and overlays:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:53:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:54:- use `src/components/shared/AppCard.tsx` instead of importing AntD `Card`
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:55:  directly for user-facing Card surfaces
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:56:- use `src/components/shared/AppDrawer.tsx` instead of importing AntD `Drawer`
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:57:  directly
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:58:- use `src/components/shared/AppModal.tsx` instead of importing AntD `Modal`
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:59:  directly
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:60:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:61:Reason:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:62:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:63:- shared wrappers provide stable project classes for surface and overlay theme
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:64:  rules
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:65:- theme presets can scope contextual surface styling to stable hooks such as
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:66:  `.app-card`, while preserving AntD child component defaults unless there is a
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:67:  confirmed product reason to override them
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:68:- transparent or glass-like themes can flicker if overlay motion fades the
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:69:  surface on the first frame
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:70:- this is an overlay behavior rule, not only a styling rule
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:71:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:72:If a new theme changes overlay surface behavior, verify the first visible frame
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:73:of the overlay, not only the final open state.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:74:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:75:Do not create theme-named wrappers such as `LiquidGlassCard` or
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:76:`CustomThemeACard`. Keep one role-based wrapper and let each theme preset own
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:77:the visual expression.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:78:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:79:## AntD MCP Usage
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:80:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:81:Use MCP when:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:82:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:83:- Starting the required AntD component API mapping for visible UI work.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:84:- A component prop is uncertain.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:85:- A demo pattern is needed.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:86:- A design token is needed.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:87:- Semantic DOM hooks are needed.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:88:- A component has changed across versions.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:89:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:90:If MCP is unavailable:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:91:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:92:- Read `https://ant.design/llms-full.txt`.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:93:- Read single component markdown, for example:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:94:  - `https://ant.design/components/button.md`
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:95:  - `https://ant.design/components/form.md`
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:96:  - `https://ant.design/components/table.md`
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:97:- Read semantic docs where relevant, for example:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:98:  - `https://ant.design/components/button/semantic.md`
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:99:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:100:## Theme Implementation Rule
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:101:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:102:`src/`ê°€ ì•„ì§ ìƒì„±ë˜ì§€ ì•Šì€ pre-implementation ìƒíƒœì—ì„œëŠ” ì•„ëž˜ êµ¬ì¡°ê°€ "ìƒì„± ì‹œ
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:103:ë”°ë¼ì•¼ í•  ëª©í‘œ êµ¬ì¡°"ìž…ë‹ˆë‹¤. `src/`ê°€ ìƒì„±ëœ ì´í›„ì—ëŠ” ë‹¨ì¼ **public theme entry
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:104:point**ë¥¼ ìœ ì§€í•˜ë˜, ì±…ìž„ì— ë”°ë¼ ë‚´ë¶€ í…Œë§ˆ íŒŒì¼ì„ ë¶„ë¦¬í•˜ëŠ” ê²ƒì„ ê¶Œìž¥í•©ë‹ˆë‹¤.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:105:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:106:Target structure:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:107:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:108:```text
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:109:src/theme/
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:110:  index.ts
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:111:  registry.ts
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:112:  create-theme.ts
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:113:  global/
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:114:  components/
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:115:  presets/
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:116:```
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:117:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:118:Rules:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:119:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:120:- `index.ts` is the public entry point.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:121:- `registry.ts` registers available named themes.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:122:- `presets/` contains optional theme-specific overrides and, when AntD tokens are
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:123:  insufficient, preset-owned structural global styles. The default preset should
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:124:  stay close to empty so the app uses stock Ant Design decisions first.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:125:- `global/` contains shared seed tokens and algorithm helpers.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:126:- `components/` contains shared component token rules only when the project has a
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:127:  documented reason to deviate from Ant Design defaults.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:128:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:129:This theme system should still centrally own:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:130:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:131:- `token`
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:132:- `components`
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:133:- optional algorithm choice
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:134:- optional preset-owned global styles
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:135:- font family
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:136:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:137:Do not spread theme decisions across unrelated components.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:138:See `08-theme-architecture.md` for the project-specific theme structure.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:139:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:140:### CSS Variable Scoping â€” Mandatory Enforcement
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:141:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:142:Read this section before writing any `--app-*` variable assignment. These rules
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:143:are machine-enforced by `scripts/ai-workflow-check.mjs`. Violations produce
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:144:silent production failures.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:145:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:146:**Five prohibited patterns:**
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:147:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:148:1. Setting `--app-*` via `style={}` on any React component other than the root
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:149:   `html` element. Portal-rendered AntD components target `document.body` by
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:150:   default and cannot inherit CSS variables from a React subtree.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:151:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:152:2. Assigning `--app-*: var(--ant-*)`. AntD CSS variables are generated by
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:153:   client-side JS and are absent during SSR. The chain has no resolved value on
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:154:   the server, causing flash-of-unstyled-content.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:155:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:156:3. Using bare `@theme { --color-x: var(--app-x) }` in Tailwind v4. Use
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:157:   `@theme inline { ... }` instead to avoid an extra variable indirection layer.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:158:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:159:4. Setting `cssVar: { key: 'name' }` and expecting CSS variable names to change.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:160:   `key` is a cache identifier only. Use `cssVar: { prefix: 'name' }` to rename
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:161:   generated CSS variable prefixes.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:162:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:163:5. Computing `activeTheme` or calling `getAppTheme()` at module level outside a
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:164:   React component. Module-level evaluation runs once at import time and does not
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:165:   react to state changes, blocking runtime dark mode switching.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:166:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:167:**Required architecture:**
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:168:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:169:```
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:170:AntD tokens
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:171:  â†’ resolved actual values  (computed server-side from active ThemeConfig)
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:172:  â†’ --app-* on html element (app/layout.tsx, <html style={...}>)
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:173:  â†’ @theme inline           (src/styles/global.css)
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:174:  â†’ Tailwind utilities
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:175:```
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:176:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:177:**Before writing any `--app-*` assignment, confirm all five:**
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:178:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:179:1. Target is `html` or `:root`.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:180:2. Value is a resolved actual value, not a `var(--ant-*)` chain.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:181:3. Value is available on first render (present in `<html style={...}>` from `layout.tsx`).
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:182:4. Tailwind bridging uses `@theme inline`.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:183:5. Theme appearance is in React state, not module scope.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:184:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:185:Do not proceed to component implementation if any of the five conditions cannot
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:186:be confirmed. If `tests/theme/theme-contract.test.ts` asserts a `var(--ant-*)`
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:187:chain while the new architecture is in place, update the test in the same commit.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:188:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:189:## CSS Rule
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:190:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:191:Custom CSS should not compete with AntD's component system.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:192:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:193:Allowed:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:194:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:195:- app shell layout
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:196:- page-level responsive layout
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:197:- local font registration
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:198:- domain-specific editor/exam surface sizing
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:199:- print/export styling when needed
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:200:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:201:Avoid:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:202:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:203:- rebuilding AntD buttons
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:204:- rebuilding AntD inputs
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:205:- custom dropdowns
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:206:- custom modals
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:207:- custom table behavior
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:208:- arbitrary color/radius/shadow values
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:209:- visual inline styles that redraw AntD component surfaces or interaction states
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:210:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:211:## Verification Rule
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:212:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:213:For UI work, verify:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:214:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:215:- desktop viewport
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:216:- mobile viewport
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:217:- no horizontal overflow
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:218:- no overlapping text
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:219:- loading state
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:220:- empty state
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:221:- error state
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:222:- primary workflow interaction
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:223:- console errors
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:224:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:225:When a dev server is available, use browser verification and screenshots.
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:226:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:227:## Conflict Rule
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:228:
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:229:If old docs mention shadcn/ui or Tailwind as the UI system but the current user
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:230:direction says Ant Design, follow Ant Design for new UI work. Keep old docs as
docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md:231:product context unless the user asks to rewrite them.
