docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:7:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:8:This file explains how theme configuration is organized in the TALKPIK AI codebase.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:9:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:10:Use this document when:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:11:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:12:- adding a new named theme such as `retro`, `glass`, or `pink`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:13:- changing global Ant Design tokens
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:14:- changing component-specific Ant Design tokens
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:15:- importing values from Ant Design Theme Editor
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:16:- deciding whether a visual value belongs in AntD global tokens, component tokens, or plain layout CSS
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:17:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:18:## Reading Gate
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:19:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:20:Read this document before work that may change how the app looks or where theme
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:21:decisions are stored.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:22:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:23:This is required for:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:24:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:25:- new pages, route screens, and workspace views
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:26:- reusable UI components
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:27:- Ant Design component customization
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:28:- changes to colors, font, radius, spacing, shadows, or surface styling
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:29:- app shell layout styling
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:30:- new visual states such as loading, empty, error, success, selected, or disabled
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:31:- any work that may introduce hardcoded visual values
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:32:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:33:This is usually not required for:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:34:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:35:- pure business logic with no UI output
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:36:- API, database, or server-only changes
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:37:- test-only changes that do not alter UI behavior
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:38:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:39:Fallback order:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:40:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:41:1. `docs/ant-design/06-ai-development-workflow.md`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:42:2. `docs/ant-design/02-global-styles.md`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:43:3. `https://ant.design/docs/react/customize-theme`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:44:4. `https://ant.design/theme-editor`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:45:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:46:If local docs conflict, follow the newest project-specific theme structure and
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:47:update the conflicting local docs before finalizing work.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:48:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:49:## Core Principle
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:50:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:51:This project now follows an AntD-first theme rule.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:52:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:53:That means:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:54:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:55:- start from stock Ant Design light and dark behavior
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:56:- keep the default preset close to empty
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:57:- add token overrides only when the product has a concrete reason
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:58:- do not restate Ant Design defaults inside our own theme files
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:59:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:60:Think of the theme system as a switchboard, not a repaint tool.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:61:Its first job is to select light or dark mode.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:62:Its second job is to hold only the overrides the product truly needs.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:63:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:64:## Why This Exists
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:65:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:66:Ant Design supports two important theme layers:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:67:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:68:1. Global theme values
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:69:   - configured through `theme.token`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:70:   - includes brand color, font, radius, and global surface decisions
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:71:2. Component theme values
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:72:   - configured through `theme.components`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:73:   - includes adjustments for specific components such as `Button`, `Menu`, `Layout`, and `Table`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:74:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:75:The TALKPIK AI project follows that model directly.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:76:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:77:We do **not** keep all theme decisions in one long file.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:78:We keep:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:79:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:80:- one public theme entry point
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:81:- one registry of available theme presets
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:82:- one preset file per theme
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:83:- separate global and component shared rules
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:84:- a default preset that stays close to stock Ant Design
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:85:- optional preset-owned global styles for theme-specific structure that AntD
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:86:  tokens cannot express
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:87:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:88:## Current Theme Folder
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:89:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:90:```text
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:91:src/theme/
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:92:  index.ts
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:93:  themes.ts
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:94:  registry.ts
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:95:  create-theme.ts
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:96:  types.ts
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:97:  antdTheme.ts
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:98:  tailwind-bridge.ts
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:99:  global/
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:100:    algorithms.ts
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:101:    shared-seed.ts
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:102:  components/
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:103:    shared.ts
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:104:  presets/
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:105:    default.ts
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:106:```
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:107:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:108:## File Responsibilities
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:109:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:110:### Public entry points
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:111:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:112:- `src/theme/index.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:113:  - the main import entry for theme APIs
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:114:  - new code should import from here
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:115:- `src/theme/themes.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:116:  - compatibility re-export
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:117:  - kept so old imports do not break immediately
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:118:- `src/theme/antdTheme.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:119:  - helper that exposes the default AntD theme config
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:120:  - acceptable for static use, but app runtime theme selection should use `getAppTheme`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:121:- `src/theme/tailwind-bridge.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:122:  - maps the approved subset of active AntD tokens to project CSS variables that
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:123:    Tailwind utilities may consume
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:124:  - must not define a second brand palette, radius scale, shadow scale, or font
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:125:    stack
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:126:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:127:### Theme assembly
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:128:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:129:- `src/theme/registry.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:130:  - registers available theme presets
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:131:  - exposes `themes`, `themePresets`, `defaultThemeName`, `defaultAppearance`, and `getAppTheme`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:132:- `src/theme/create-theme.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:133:  - builds the final `ThemeConfig` from shared rules plus a preset
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:134:  - merges global token values and component token values
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:135:  - carries optional preset-owned global styles into the active theme definition
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:136:  - enables or preserves AntD CSS-variable output when supported by the chosen
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:137:    AntD version
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:138:  - applies the light or dark AntD algorithm automatically
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:139:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:140:### Shared theme inputs
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:141:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:142:- `src/theme/global/shared-seed.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:143:  - shared global seed tokens
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:144:  - keep this minimal and neutral
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:145:  - right now this is mainly the app font family
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:146:- `src/theme/global/algorithms.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:147:  - maps `light` and `dark` appearance to Ant Design algorithms
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:148:- `src/theme/components/shared.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:149:  - shared component-level overrides used across all presets
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:150:  - keep this empty unless the project has a documented reason to deviate from Ant Design defaults
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:151:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:152:### Theme presets
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:153:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:154:- `src/theme/presets/default.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:155:  - the current default theme preset
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:156:  - should stay close to empty
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:157:  - its job is to say "use light mode" or "use dark mode", not to restate Ant Design defaults
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:158:- `src/theme/presets/liquid-glass.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:159:  - owns both the Liquid Glass AntD token overrides and the Liquid Glass
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:160:    structural global styles
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:161:  - keep pseudo-elements, backdrop filters, overlay first-frame rules, and
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:162:    Liquid Glass CSS variables here instead of in `src/styles/global.css`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:163:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:164:### Types
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:165:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:166:- `src/theme/types.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:167:  - shared types for appearances, presets, and built theme definitions
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:168:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:169:## Theme Flow
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:170:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:171:The runtime theme flow is:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:172:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:173:1. a preset such as `defaultThemePreset` defines optional appearance-specific overrides
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:174:2. `createThemeFamily` builds final `ThemeConfig` objects for `light` and `dark`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:175:3. `registry.ts` exposes the available themes
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:176:4. the app root calls `getAppTheme(themeName, appearance)`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:177:5. `ConfigProvider` receives `activeTheme.antd`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:178:6. the theme bridge resolves AntD token values server-side and injects them as
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:179:   `--app-*` CSS variables on the `html` element in `app/layout.tsx`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:180:7. Tailwind utilities read only those `--app-*` variables via `@theme inline`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:181:   in `src/styles/global.css`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:182:8. new UI reads AntD tokens directly at render time when component logic needs
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:183:   token values
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:184:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:185:This gives the app one source of truth at runtime even though the files are split
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:186:for maintainability. The `--app-*` variables on `html` ensure portal-rendered
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:187:AntD components (Modal, Drawer, Notification) inherit the correct values.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:188:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:189:## AntD And Tailwind Synchronization
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:190:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:191:Tailwind is a utility layer, not a parallel design system. The source of truth
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:192:for visual decisions remains the active Ant Design theme.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:193:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:194:Synchronization rule:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:195:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:196:- AntD `theme.token` owns brand color, font family, radius, base surfaces,
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:197:  semantic colors, shadows, and motion-level decisions.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:198:- AntD `theme.components` owns component-family customization such as Button,
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:199:  Menu, Layout, Table, Tabs, Form, Drawer, and Modal adjustments.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:200:- `tailwind-bridge.ts` may expose only a small approved subset of resolved AntD
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:201:  tokens as project CSS variables.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:202:- Tailwind configuration or CSS may alias utilities to those variables, but must
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:203:  not contain copied hex colors, copied radius values, copied shadows, or a
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:204:  separate font stack.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:205:- Tailwind classes are allowed for layout composition, responsive behavior,
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:206:  width/height constraints, grid/flex helpers, and small spacing adjustments.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:207:- Tailwind classes should not be used to restyle AntD component internals when
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:208:  an AntD prop, variant, token, or component token can express the change.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:209:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:210:### CSS Variable Architecture Contract
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:211:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:212:> **Required reading gate.** Read this section before writing any `--app-*`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:213:> variable assignment. These constraints prevent two classes of silent production
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:214:> bugs: portal color failures and SSR flash-of-unstyled-content.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:215:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:216:#### Rule 1 â€” Scope `--app-*` on `html` or `:root` only
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:217:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:218:`--app-*` variables must be declared on `html` or `:root`. They must not be
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:219:declared via `style={}` on any React component below `html`.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:220:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:221:AntD `Modal`, `Drawer`, `Notification`, and `Tooltip` render via portals that
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:222:target `document.body` by default. Those portal elements sit outside any React
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:223:component subtree. A `style={}` prop on a wrapper div creates a CSS inheritance
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:224:boundary; portal-rendered content lives outside that boundary and receives no
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:225:value for any variable declared only on the wrapper.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:226:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:227:Exception: `getContainer={false}` and custom `getContainer` props attach the
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:228:overlay to the current DOM position instead of `body`. If every overlay in the
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:229:project uses this prop, portal inheritance is not a concern. Do not rely on this
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:230:exception unless it is explicitly documented per-component.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:231:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:232:```tsx
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:233:/* REQUIRED â€” portals always inherit from html/root */
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:234:// app/layout.tsx (server component)
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:235:<html style={{ '--app-color-primary': resolvedToken.colorPrimary } as React.CSSProperties}>
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:236:```
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:237:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:238:#### Rule 2 â€” `--app-*` must hold resolved actual values, not CSS variable chains
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:239:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:240:`--app-*` variables must hold resolved actual values (hex, px, font stack, or
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:241:box-shadow value). They must not reference `--ant-*` via `var()`.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:242:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:243:AntD CSS variables (`--ant-*`) are emitted by client-side JavaScript when
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:244:`ConfigProvider` mounts. They do not exist in any CSS context during server-side
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:245:rendering. A chain like `--app-color-primary: var(--ant-color-primary)` resolves
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:246:to an empty/invalid value on the first server render, causing visible flash.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:247:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:248:```css
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:249:/* PROHIBITED â€” --ant-* does not exist at SSR time */
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:250:--app-color-primary: var(--ant-color-primary);
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:251:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:252:/* REQUIRED â€” resolved actual value, safe at SSR */
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:253:--app-color-primary: #1677ff;
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:254:```
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:255:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:256:Resolved values must be injected server-side. The recommended pattern is a
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:257:server component in `app/layout.tsx` that reads a theme cookie via `cookies()`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:258:from `next/headers` and writes resolved token values to `<html style={...}>`.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:259:Note: calling `cookies()` makes the route dynamically rendered.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:260:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:261:When a docs or code change affects `--app-*` variable values, update
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:262:`tests/theme/theme-contract.test.ts` in the same commit. A contract test that
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:263:asserts a `var(--ant-*)` chain while the docs prohibit it is a contradiction that
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:264:will mislead future agents.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:265:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:266:#### Rule 3 â€” Use `@theme inline` in Tailwind v4
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:267:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:268:In Tailwind v4, `@theme { --color-x: var(--app-x) }` generates utilities that
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:269:reference `var(--color-x)`, creating an extra indirection layer. Use
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:270:`@theme inline` instead: it writes `var(--app-x)` directly into each generated
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:271:utility class.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:272:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:273:```css
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:274:/* REQUIRED in Tailwind v4 */
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:275:@theme inline {
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:276:  --color-primary: var(--app-color-primary);
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:277:  /* produces: .bg-primary { background-color: var(--app-color-primary) } */
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:278:}
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:279:```
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:280:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:281:#### Rule 4 â€” `cssVar.prefix` controls CSS variable names; `cssVar.key` does not
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:282:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:283:In AntD v6, `cssVar.key` is a deduplication cache identifier only. It does not
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:284:change generated CSS variable names. The prefix that produces `--ant-color-primary`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:285:is `cssVar.prefix`, which defaults to `ant`.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:286:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:287:To change variable names from `--ant-*` to a custom prefix, set
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:288:`cssVar: { prefix: 'myapp', key: 'myapp' }` explicitly.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:289:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:290:#### Correct data flow (one direction only)
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:291:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:292:```
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:293:AntD design tokens
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:294:  â†’ resolved actual values  (server-side, from active ThemeConfig)
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:295:  â†’ --app-* on html element (app/layout.tsx via <html style={...}>)
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:296:  â†’ @theme inline           (src/styles/global.css)
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:297:  â†’ Tailwind utilities
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:298:```
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:299:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:300:#### Bridge targets
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:301:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:302:The following `--app-*` variables are the approved bridge set. Each must hold a
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:303:resolved actual value injected from `layout.tsx`:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:304:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:305:```
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:306:--app-color-primary        resolved hex (e.g. #1677ff)
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:307:--app-color-bg-layout      resolved hex
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:308:--app-color-bg-container   resolved hex
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:309:--app-color-text           resolved hex
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:310:--app-color-text-secondary resolved hex
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:311:--app-color-border         resolved hex
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:312:--app-radius               resolved px (e.g. 6px)
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:313:--app-font-family          resolved font stack
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:314:--app-shadow-elevated      resolved box-shadow value
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:315:```
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:316:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:317:Generate these values from `src/theme/` token resolution at request time, not by
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:318:hand. Do not add new bridge variables unless the token has a documented use in
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:319:Tailwind utilities or plain CSS rules outside AntD components.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:320:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:321:Automated checks for the rules above are enforced by `scripts/ai-workflow-check.mjs`.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:322:When adding or changing `--app-*` declarations, run the checker before marking
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:323:work complete.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:324:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:325:## Overlay Surface Rule
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:326:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:327:`Card`, `Drawer`, and `Modal` are not just visual values. They are shared app
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:328:surfaces that themes may need to style in context.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:329:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:330:`Drawer` and `Modal` also have an opening lifecycle.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:331:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:332:For themes with transparent, blurred, or glass-like surfaces, the first frame
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:333:of an overlay matters. If the overlay motion fades the surface on entry, users
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:334:can see a weak/default-looking surface before the themed surface is fully
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:335:visible.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:336:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:337:Treat that as an overlay behavior rule, not only a token rule.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:338:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:339:Project rule:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:340:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:341:- user-facing `Card` surfaces should use `src/components/shared/AppCard.tsx`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:342:  instead of importing AntD `Card` directly
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:343:- user-facing `Drawer` components should use `src/components/shared/AppDrawer.tsx`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:344:- user-facing `Modal` components should use `src/components/shared/AppModal.tsx`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:345:- those wrappers provide stable project classes for surface and overlay theme
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:346:  behavior
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:347:- theme presets still own the visual values
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:348:- preset-owned global styles may provide structural layers such as blur or
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:349:  highlight when AntD tokens cannot express them
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:350:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:351:In short:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:352:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:353:- tokens decide the material
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:354:- shared wrappers provide stable hooks such as `.app-card`, `.app-surface`,
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:355:  `.app-drawer`, and `.app-modal`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:356:- preset-owned global styles decide extra surface structure only when AntD
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:357:  tokens are not enough
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:358:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:359:Do not create theme-named component forks such as `LiquidGlassCard` or
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:360:`CustomThemeACard`. Create one role-based shared wrapper such as `AppCard`, then
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:361:let each theme preset style that stable hook.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:362:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:363:When child AntD components need to adapt to a themed surface, preserve AntD
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:364:defaults first. If a confirmed product requirement needs a contextual override,
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:365:scope that rule to the surface hook:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:366:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:367:```css
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:368:html[data-theme='liquidGlass'] .app-card .ant-tag {
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:369:  /* Liquid Glass Card context only */
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:370:}
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:371:```
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:372:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:373:This keeps the component API stable while allowing each preset to define how
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:374:inputs, tags, or other AntD children behave inside the surface. Do not add
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:375:Card-scoped Button styling unless the request explicitly calls for a non-default
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:376:Button appearance.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:377:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:378:## Global vs Component Rules
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:379:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:380:Use this decision rule:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:381:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:382:### Put a value in global tokens when it changes the app's overall design language
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:383:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:384:Examples:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:385:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:386:- `colorPrimary`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:387:- `fontFamily`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:388:- `borderRadius`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:389:- `colorBgLayout`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:390:- `colorBgContainer`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:391:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:392:### Put a value in component tokens when it only changes one AntD component family
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:393:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:394:Examples:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:395:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:396:- `Button.primaryShadow`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:397:- `Menu.itemSelectedBg`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:398:- `Layout.headerBg`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:399:- `Table.rowHoverBg`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:400:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:401:### Put a value in plain layout CSS only when AntD is not expressing that concern
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:402:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:403:Examples:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:404:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:405:- a custom print rule
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:406:- a canvas-like editor sizing rule
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:407:- a layout-only spacing helper that is not a theme decision
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:408:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:409:Do not create a theme override just to restate what Ant Design already does.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:410:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:411:## How To Add A New Theme
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:412:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:413:Example target:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:414:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:415:- `retro`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:416:- `glass`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:417:- `black`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:418:- `pink`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:419:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:420:Steps:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:421:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:422:1. Create a new preset file in `src/theme/presets/`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:423:   - example: `src/theme/presets/retro.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:424:2. Export a preset object that satisfies `AppThemePreset<'retro'>`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:425:3. Define only the `light` and `dark` overrides that the new theme really needs
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:426:4. Register the preset in `src/theme/registry.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:427:5. If the user should be able to choose it, extend theme selection state and settings UI
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:428:6. Verify desktop and mobile layouts after switching themes
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:429:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:430:Example shape:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:431:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:432:```ts
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:433:import type { AppThemePreset } from '../types';
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:434:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:435:export const retroThemePreset = {
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:436:  name: 'retro',
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:437:  label: 'Retro',
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:438:  description: 'Warm and editorial theme',
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:439:  appearances: {
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:440:    light: {
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:441:      token: {
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:442:        colorPrimary: '#b45309',
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:443:      },
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:444:    },
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:445:    dark: {
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:446:      token: {
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:447:        colorPrimary: '#f59e0b',
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:448:      },
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:449:    },
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:450:  },
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:451:} satisfies AppThemePreset<'retro'>;
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:452:```
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:453:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:454:## How To Use Ant Design Theme Editor
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:455:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:456:Reference:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:457:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:458:- `https://ant.design/theme-editor`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:459:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:460:Use Theme Editor as a design tool, not as a dump target for every possible token.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:461:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:462:Recommended workflow:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:463:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:464:1. Start with stock Ant Design defaults
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:465:2. Use Theme Editor only when the product has a concrete branding or component requirement
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:466:3. Export the values
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:467:4. Move the relevant global values into `token`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:468:5. Move component-specific values into `components`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:469:6. Keep only the overrides that are actually needed by the project
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:470:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:471:Do **not** blindly paste every exported value if many of them are just derived alias tokens.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:472:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:473:Prefer this order:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:474:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:475:1. keep shared structural rules in `global/shared-seed.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:476:2. keep the default preset nearly empty
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:477:3. keep per-theme brand values in a preset file only when the product actually needs them
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:478:4. keep per-component theme overrides in `components` or in the preset's `components` block only when Ant Design defaults are not enough
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:479:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:480:## Rules For Future Edits
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:481:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:482:- New theme work should import from `src/theme/index.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:483:- Do not turn `registry.ts` into a long token file
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:484:- Do not put all preset values back into one giant `themes.ts`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:485:- Do not duplicate the same color in three places without reason
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:486:- Do not add visual values to Tailwind unless they reference the project
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:487:  `--app-*` bridge variables or are layout-only values with no design-token
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:488:  meaning
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:489:- If a shared component token starts growing large, split it into per-component files under `src/theme/components/`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:490:- If a preset becomes large, keep its values inside the preset file instead of spreading them across random UI components
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:491:- Do not add a preset override just to make Ant Design look the same as it already does by default
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:492:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:493:## Review Checklist For Theme Changes
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:494:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:495:### CSS Variable Gate (check these first)
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:496:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:497:These items catch structural defects. Do not proceed to the remaining checklist
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:498:until all of these pass.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:499:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:500:- [ ] All `--app-*` CSS variables are declared on `html` or `:root` (via
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:501:      `app/layout.tsx`). No `--app-*` variable is set through `style={}` on a
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:502:      React component.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:503:- [ ] No `--app-*` variable holds a `var(--ant-*)` chain. Every `--app-*`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:504:      variable holds a resolved actual value (hex, px, font stack, or
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:505:      box-shadow).
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:506:- [ ] Resolved `--app-*` values are injected server-side so they are present on
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:507:      first render before any client JS runs.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:508:- [ ] `src/styles/global.css` uses `@theme inline { ... }` (not bare `@theme`)
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:509:      for Tailwind v4 variable bridging.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:510:- [ ] Active theme appearance is stored in React state or context, not computed
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:511:      at module level outside a React component.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:512:- [ ] `tests/theme/theme-contract.test.ts` is consistent with the current
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:513:      `--app-*` values and data flow (no assertion of a `var(--ant-*)` chain if
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:514:      the new architecture is in place).
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:515:- [ ] Verified in a running build that a portal-rendered AntD component (Modal
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:516:      or Drawer) displays the correct `--app-color-primary` color.
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:517:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:518:### General Checks
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:519:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:520:Before calling theme work complete, verify:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:521:
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:522:- the app still works with stock Ant Design light and dark algorithms
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:523:- global changes still come from AntD tokens
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:524:- component-specific overrides still live in `theme.components`
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:525:- Tailwind utilities consume project bridge variables instead of copied theme
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:526:  values
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:527:- AntD components and Tailwind-authored surfaces still match after switching
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:528:  light/dark appearance
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:529:- the default preset is still close to empty unless there is a documented reason otherwise
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:530:- dark mode still uses AntD dark algorithm unless there is a documented reason otherwise
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:531:- no new hardcoded color values were scattered into unrelated components
docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:532:- desktop and mobile layouts still render correctly

codex
## 1. antd 호환성 fix 검수

### ✅ 정합 항목
- 원인 진단은 맞습니다. `antd/es/index.js:1`, `antd/es/theme/index.js:1` 모두 `"use client"`이고, `antd/es/theme/context.js:13`에서 `React.createContext(...)`를 module top-level에서 만듭니다. 서버 컴포넌트에서 `antd` theme namespace를 평가하면 문제 날 수 있습니다.
- `tailwind-bridge.ts` 자체는 런타임 `antd` import를 제거했습니다. 현재는 `import type { ThemeConfig } from "antd"`만 있고 값 import는 없습니다: `src/theme/tailwind-bridge.ts:17`.
- light/dark 주요 색상 fallback은 대체로 antd 6.4.3 소스와 맞습니다. 예: seed `colorPrimary`는 `node_modules/antd/es/theme/themes/seed.js:23`, `borderRadius`는 `:52`, light neutral 값은 `default/colors.js:26-42`, dark neutral 값은 `dark/colors.js:28-44` 근거입니다.
- `layout.tsx`는 appearance cookie를 읽고 `getResolvedBridgeVarsByAppearance(appearance)`를 `<html style>`에 넣습니다: `src/app/layout.tsx:31-50`.

### ❌ 문제 항목
- **서버 import 차단이 아직 불완전합니다.** `layout.tsx`가 여전히 `@/theme`에서 `getAppTheme`를 import하고 호출합니다: `src/app/layout.tsx:7-11`, `:41`. 이 경로는 `src/theme/index.ts:1-4` → `registry.ts:1` → `create-theme.ts:3` → `"use client"` `algorithms.ts:1,7,11-13`로 이어집니다. 즉 서버 layout이 아직 client-marked antd theme 경로를 밟습니다. 게다가 `theme` 변수는 사용되지 않습니다.
- **기존 테스트와 backward compatibility가 깨져 있습니다.** `getResolvedBridgeVars(themeConfig)`는 deprecated라지만 항상 light만 반환합니다: `src/theme/tailwind-bridge.ts:69-72`. 그런데 테스트는 여전히 이 API로 dark 값을 검증합니다: `tests/theme/theme-contract.test.ts:38-49`. 현재 구현이면 dark도 `#FFFFFF`를 돌려 테스트가 실패합니다.
- **hex 대소문자 때문에 기존 테스트도 실패합니다.** 테스트는 `#1677ff`, `#ffffff`, `#d9d9d9`를 `toBe`로 기대합니다: `tests/theme/theme-contract.test.ts:30-32`. 구현은 `#1677FF`, `#FFFFFF`, `#D9D9D9`입니다: `src/theme/tailwind-bridge.ts:30-35`.
- **dark shadow fallback은 antd dark token과 불일치합니다.** 구현은 light/dark 모두 같은 검은 shadow를 씁니다: `src/theme/tailwind-bridge.ts:26-27`, `:38`, `:50`. 하지만 antd dark neutral은 `colorShadow`를 `rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2)`로 둡니다: `node_modules/antd/es/theme/themes/dark/colors.js:20-23`, shadow alias는 이 값을 기반으로 계산합니다: `node_modules/antd/es/theme/util/alias.js:24-26`, `:121-125`.

### ⚠️ 의심·후속 작업
- 문서 계약과 충돌합니다. 문서는 bridge 값을 “request time token resolution”에서 만들라고 합니다: `docs/ant-design/08-theme-architecture.md:317-318`. 이번 hardcoded fallback은 현실적 우회지만, 예외 사유와 테스트 갱신이 필요합니다.
- 현재 brand override는 없습니다. default preset은 비어 있습니다: `src/theme/presets/default.ts:7-10`, shared seed는 font만 덮습니다: `src/theme/global/shared-seed.ts:6-8`. 그래서 FOUC 위험은 “현재는 낮음, future preset 추가 시 높음”입니다.

## 2. pg_cron 마이그레이션 검수

### ✅ 정합 항목
- idempotency 방향은 맞습니다. 기존 job을 `cron.job`에서 찾고: `supabase/migrations/20260527110000_register_cleanup_cron.sql:34-38`, 있으면 `cron.unschedule(v_jobid)` 후 재등록합니다: `:40-50`.
- pg_cron 미설치 환경은 fail 없이 빠집니다. `pg_extension` 확인 후 notice + `return`: `:28-31`.
- 스케줄 표현식은 `0 4 * * *`입니다: `:46-49`. Supabase 문서는 cron 예시를 GMT 기준으로 설명하고, Supabase DB 기본 timezone도 UTC라고 설명합니다. 근거: Supabase Cron quickstart, Supabase database configuration docs.
- 함수 기본 인자 호출은 의도대로입니다. cron command는 `private.cleanup_unconfirmed_users()`이고: `:49`, 함수 default는 `retention_days=30`, `dry_run=false`, `max_batch=1000`입니다: `supabase/migrations/20260526180000_cleanup_unconfirmed_users.sql:22-25`.
- 함수는 `SECURITY DEFINER` + locked `search_path`입니다: `20260526180000_cleanup_unconfirmed_users.sql:27-30`, public/anon/authenticated 실행권한도 revoke합니다: `:75-76`.

### ❌ 문제 항목
- 기능 자체의 치명 버그는 못 찾았습니다.

### ⚠️ 의심·후속 작업
- `pg_cron` extension을 직접 활성화하지 않습니다. 의도적으로 skip 설계한 것은 맞지만, fresh cloud DB에서 extension이 꺼져 있으면 job은 등록되지 않습니다: `20260527110000_register_cleanup_cron.sql:28-31`. 운영 체크가 필요합니다.
- 마이그레이션 인덱스가 갱신되지 않았습니다. `supabase/migrations/INDEX.md`는 새 migration 추가 시 INDEX 갱신을 요구합니다: `supabase/migrations/INDEX.md:64-71`, `:80`. 현재 grep 결과 새 `20260527110000_register_cleanup_cron.sql` 항목은 없습니다.
- 권한은 “마이그레이션이 postgres급 role로 실행된다”는 전제에 기대고 있습니다. 파일 주석도 이 전제를 적고 있습니다: `20260527110000_register_cleanup_cron.sql:15-17`. Supabase CLI/Cloud migration 경로에서는 보통 맞지만, 제한 role로 직접 적용하면 `cron.job` 조회나 `cron.schedule`에서 실패할 수 있습니다.

## 3. 종합 판정

- **결론**: **FAIL**
- **antd fix 점수**: **5/10**  
  원인 방향은 맞지만 서버 import 경로가 아직 남아 있고, 기존 테스트가 깨집니다.
- **pg_cron fix 점수**: **8/10**  
  기능 SQL은 대체로 맞습니다. 다만 extension 활성화 운영 확인과 migration index 누락이 남았습니다.
- **한 줄 추천**: 머지 보류. 먼저 `layout.tsx`에서 `getAppTheme/@/theme` 서버 import를 제거하고, theme bridge 테스트를 새 API 기준으로 고친 뒤 머지하세요.
